// --- ARCHIVO: C:\Users\WinterOS\Desktop\Transcaribe\Transcaribe_APP\target\classes\templates\usuarios\auth\registro.html ---
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org">
<head>
  <link rel="stylesheet" th:href="@{/css/usuario.css}" />
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Registro - Transcaribe</title>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="page">
    <div th:replace="~{fragments/header :: header}"></div>

    <main class="center-card">
      <div class="card login-card"> 
        <h1 class="title">Crear cuenta</h1>

        <form th:action="@{/registro}" method="post" class="form" id="registroForm">
          <input type="text" name="username" placeholder="Nombre completo" required />
          <input type="email" name="correo" placeholder="Correo electrónico" required />
          
          <input type="password" id="password" name="password" placeholder="Contraseña" required minlength="8" />
          
          <input type="password" id="confirmPassword" placeholder="Confirmar contraseña" required />
          <p id="passwordMessage" class="match-error">Las contraseñas no coinciden</p>
          
          <input type="text" 
                 id="numeroTarjeta"
                 name="numeroTarjeta" 
                 placeholder="Número de Tarjeta (10 dígitos)" 
                 maxlength="10" 
                 inputmode="numeric"
                 pattern="\d{10}"
                 title="El número de tarjeta debe tener exactamente 10 dígitos numéricos"
                 required />

          <button class="primary" type="submit" id="btnRegistrar">Registrar</button>
        </form>

        <div class="register-row" style="margin-top: 20px;"> 
          <span style="color: rgb(95, 93, 93); font-size: 0.9rem;">¿Ya tienes cuenta?</span>
          <a th:href="@{/}" class="ghost" style="padding: 4px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 8px;">Inicia sesión</a>
        </div>

        <div style="margin-top: 15px;">
            <p class="error" th:if="${error}" th:text="${error}" 
               style="background: rgba(255,0,0,0.2); color: #ffcccc; padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 0.9rem;"></p>
            <p class="success" th:if="${mensaje}" th:text="${mensaje}"
               style="background: rgba(0,255,0,0.2); color: #ccffcc; padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 0.9rem;"></p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>

    <div th:replace="~{fragments/footer :: footer}"></div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const password = document.getElementById('password');
    const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword');
    const message = document.getElementById('passwordMessage');
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnRegistrar');
    const inputTarjeta = document.getElementById('numeroTarjeta');

    function validarPassword() {
      if (confirmPassword.value === "") {
        message.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }

      if (password.value.length < 8) {
        message.textContent = "La contraseña debe tener mínimo 8 caracteres";
        message.style.display = 'block';
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.style.opacity = "0.5";
      } else if (password.value !== confirmPassword.value) {
        message.textContent = "Las contraseñas no coinciden";
        confirmPassword.classList.add('no-match');
        message.style.display = 'block';
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.style.opacity = "0.5";
      } else {
        confirmPassword.classList.remove('no-match');
        message.style.display = 'none';
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.style.opacity = "1";
      }
    }

    password.addEventListener('change', validarPassword);
    confirmPassword.addEventListener('keyup', validarPassword);

    inputTarjeta.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

// --- ARCHIVO: C:\Users\WinterOS\Desktop\Transcaribe\Transcaribe_APP\target\classes\templates\usuarios\password-recovery\olvido-password.html ---
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org">
<head>
  <link rel="stylesheet" th:href="@{/css/usuario.css}" />
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Recuperar Contraseña - Transcaribe</title>
</head>
<body class="login-page">
    <div class="page">
        <div th:replace="~{fragments/header :: header}"></div>

        <main class="center-card">
            <div class="card">
                <div class="recovery-icon">🔑</div>
                <h1 class="title">¿Olvidaste tu clave?</h1>
                
                <p class="info-text">
                    No te preocupes. Ingresa tu correo electrónico y te enviaremos un 
                    <strong>código de seguridad</strong> para restablecer tu cuenta de Transcaribe.
                </p>

                <form th:action="@{/olvido-password}" method="post" class="form">
                    <!-- Token CSRF para seguridad en Spring Security -->

                    <label for="correo">Correo Electrónico</label>
                    <input type="email" 
                           id="correo"
                           name="correo" 
                           placeholder="ejemplo@correo.com" 
                           required 
                           autocomplete="email" />

                    <button class="primary" type="submit">Enviar Código</button>
                </form>

// --- ARCHIVO: C:\Users\WinterOS\Desktop\Transcaribe\Transcaribe_APP\target\classes\templates\usuarios\password-recovery\restablecer-password.html ---
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org">
<head>
  <link rel="stylesheet" th:href="@{/css/usuario.css}" />
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Nueva Contraseña - Transcaribe</title>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="page">
        <div th:replace="~{fragments/header :: header}"></div>
        <main class="center-card">
            <div class="card">
                <h1 class="title">Crea tu nueva clave</h1>
                
                <form th:action="@{/confirmar-restablecimiento}" method="post" class="form" id="passwordForm">
                    <input type="hidden" name="correo" th:value="${correo}" />
                    <input type="hidden" name="codigo" th:value="${codigo}" />

                    <label for="nuevaPassword">Nueva Contraseña</label>
                    <input type="password" id="nuevaPassword" name="nuevaPassword" 
                           placeholder="Mínimo 8 caracteres" required minlength="8" />

                    <label for="confirmarPassword">Confirmar Contraseña</label>
                    <input type="password" id="confirmarPassword" 
                           placeholder="Repite tu contraseña" required />

                    <p id="matchMessage" style="font-size: 0.8em; margin-top: -10px; margin-bottom: 15px;"></p>

                    <button class="primary" type="submit" id="submitBtn">Actualizar Contraseña</button>
                </form>
            </div>
        </main>
        <div th:replace="~{fragments/footer :: footer}"></div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const pass = document.getElementById('nuevaPassword');
        const confirm = document.getElementById('confirmarPassword');
        const message = document.getElementById('matchMessage');
        const btn = document.getElementById('submitBtn');

        function validate() {
            if (confirm.value === "") {
                message.textContent = "";
                btn.disabled = false;
            } else if (pass.value === confirm.value) {
                message.textContent = "✓ Las contraseñas coinciden";
                message.style.color = "green";
                btn.disabled = false;
            } else {
                message.textContent = "✗ Las contraseñas no coinciden";
                message.style.color = "red";
                btn.disabled = true;
            }
        }

        pass.onchange = validate;
        confirm.onkeyup = validate;
    </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    function togglePaymentFields() {
      const method = document.querySelector('input[name="metodoPago"]:checked').value;
      const pse = document.getElementById('pseFields');
      const tc = document.getElementById('tarjetaCreditoFields');

      if (method === 'tarjetaCredito') {
        pse.style.display = 'none';
        pse.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(i => i.disabled = true);

        tc.style.display = 'block';
        tc.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(i => i.disabled = false);
      } else {
        pse.style.display = 'block';
        pse.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(i => i.disabled = false);

        tc.style.display = 'none';
        tc.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(i => i.disabled = true);
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', togglePaymentFields);

    document.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      if (e.target.id === 'numeroTarjetaPago') {
        let v = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 16);
        e.target.value = v.replace(/(.{4})/g, '$1 ').trim();
      }
      if (e.target.id === 'fechaVencimientoPago') {
        let v = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4);
        if (v.length > 2) v = v.slice(0, 2) + '/' + v.slice(2);
        e.target.value = v;
      }
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

                            th:onclick="editarTarjeta([[${tarjeta.numeroTarjeta}]])">✏️</button>

                        <input type="hidden" name="numeroTarjeta" th:value="${tarjeta.numeroTarjeta}" />

                                onclick="return confirm('¿Eliminar esta tarjeta?')">🗑️</button>
                    </form>

            </div>

                <input type="text" name="nuevoNumero" class="input-corto" 
                       placeholder="10 dígitos" pattern="\d{10}" maxlength="10" required />
                <button type="submit" class="primary" style="padding: 8px 20px;">Vincular</button>
            </form>
        </div>

  <script th:inline="javascript">
    function editarTarjeta(numeroActual) {
        const nuevoNumero = prompt("Nuevo número (10 dígitos) para reemplazar a " + numeroActual + ":", numeroActual);
        if (nuevoNumero && nuevoNumero.length === 10 && nuevoNumero !== numeroActual) {
            const form = document.createElement('form');
            form.method = 'POST';
            form.action = /*[[@{/tarjetas/editar}]]*/ '/tarjetas/editar';
            
            const inputActual = document.createElement('input');
            inputActual.type = 'hidden';
            inputActual.name = 'numeroActual';
            inputActual.value = numeroActual;
            
            const inputNuevo = document.createElement('input');
            inputNuevo.type = 'hidden';
            inputNuevo.name = 'nuevoNumero';
            inputNuevo.value = nuevoNumero;
            
            form.appendChild(inputActual);
            form.appendChild(inputNuevo);
            document.body.appendChild(form);
            form.submit();
        }
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
